  67 agree with a declaration, 3 disagree, 854 implied by declarations, 7 held up by the
  overlap alone, 71 declared but unmeasurable.

  A constant difference says the two censuses are CONSISTENT with nesting at that offset.
  It does not establish the nesting: nothing in an access census names a type. A varying
  difference is the stronger result - it is a contradiction, and the sites are named.
  TWO TYPES AT THE SAME BASE are not thereby one type: a record and its own first member
  share an address. What the closure establishes is that they are not INDEPENDENT, and
  that a layout claim about one is a layout claim about the other.
